## statenum_t

```ml
enum statenum_t
```

Assigns stable indices to all actor, weapon, pickup, and effect states referenced by gameplay metadata.


[View source](https://github.com/MiniLangProject/MiniDoom/blob/main/src/info.ml#L308)
